### [379] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理阶段边界处KV缓存重放（stage-replay）的保真度问题，实验表明精确token重放虽可重复，但保留实时缓存与一次性预填充在BF16精度下会产生分歧（166个后缀、20个正确性标签差异，准确率仅差1点），而在FP32下无解码分歧。通过双向移植48层KV缓存可使分歧轨迹跟随缓存供体，证明边界KV缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度则调节其行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段重放诊断技术假设"新鲜预填充的延续"等同于"从最初到达该前缀的解码器状态继续"，即认为精确token重放能够保持实时状态保真。作者希望在推理阶段边界审计这一假设是否成立，特别是在Qwen2.5衍生系统中。

Method: 使用匹配的200-item实验，对比保留实时缓存与一次性预填充相同整数token。设计了固定前缀2x2实验（200个token状态恒定，交叉构造方式和精度），并进行FP32与BF16对比。此外还通过前瞻性桥接（使逐token增量缓存与保留实时缓存在12/12行比特级一致）、全200项存档账本审计，以及双向移植全部48层KV层进行因果验证。

Result: BF16下两种构造在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上存在差异（准确率差1点，95%置信区间[-3.5, +5.5]）；FP32无解码分歧（Wilson上限1.88%）。比特精确的重放具有可重复性，但不保留实时状态保真度。双向移植48层KV缓存使所有测试的发散延续跟随缓存供体（主检查点24/24，后续检查点43/43）。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以可重复但不保真。在测试状态下，边界KV缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，而数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）调节其行为表达。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [380] [GVR-Coder: A Visual-Feedback Framework for Structured SVG Generation in Complex Document and Meeting Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28073)
*Yiming Xu,Jihua Kang,Chunsai Du,Qifan Zhang,Wangqiu Zhou,Yiting Wu,Tianqi Li,Qi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GVR-Coder框架，用于从长篇专业文本生成逻辑清晰、视觉美观的SVG图表。构建了DocMeetSVG-100K数据集，并通过课程驱动的拒绝采样微调、布局约束注入、双渲染反馈强化学习以及生成-验证-修复智能体循环，解决了数据稀缺、布局混乱和视觉反馈缺失三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业会议和文档审查场景中，长文本带来高认知负担，需要将其转化为逻辑清晰的图表。现有Text-to-SVG生成研究面临数据稀缺、缺乏布局先验和缺乏细粒度视觉反馈三大挑战。

Method: (1) 构建DocMeetSVG-100K大规模SVG数据集；(2) 提出GVR-Coder框架，采用课程驱动的拒绝采样微调逐步增强复杂结构建模能力；(3) 显式注入布局约束知识；(4) 引入基于双渲染反馈的强化学习，通过奖励信号联合优化结构复杂性和视觉美感；(5) 设计生成-验证-修复智能体循环提供细粒度反馈和定向改进。

Result: 大量实验表明GVR-Coder在逻辑连贯性和视觉吸引力方面优于竞争基线方法，能够可靠生成高质量的逻辑图表。

Conclusion: GVR-Coder有效解决了Text-to-SVG生成的数据、布局和反馈三大挑战，为专业文档图表自动化生成提供了可行方案，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: In demanding professional environments and meeting review scenarios, lengthy text often imposes a high cognitive load. To facilitate efficient information communication, transforming verbose text into logically clear diagrams is essential. Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) provide an effective representation for this purpose due to their editability and resolution independence. However, current research on Text-to-SVG generation remains hindered by three major challenges: (1) the scarcity of datasets for complex, logic-rich diagrams; (2) the absence of explicit layout priors, which leads to chaotic spatial arrangements; and (3) the lack of fine-grained visual feedback to validate rendered outputs and correct aesthetic defects. To address these challenges, at the data level, we introduce DocMeetSVG-100K, a large-scale SVG dataset tailored for document authoring and meeting review scenarios. At the model level, we propose GVR-Coder, a novel framework designed to generate high-quality logical diagrams from lengthy professional texts. Specifically, we adopt a curriculum-driven rejection sampling fine-tuning to progressively enhance the model's capability in modeling complex structures, while explicitly incorporating layout constraint knowledge during training. In addition, we introduce reinforcement learning from dual rendering feedback, a mechanism that provides implicit feedback through reward signals to jointly optimize structural complexity and visual aesthetics. Furthermore, we design a generate-verify-repair agent loop, which improves generation quality through explicit, fine-grained feedback and targeted refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GVR-Coder outperforms competitive baselines and reliably produces logically coherent and visually appealing diagrams. Code and data are available at https://github.com/CurryaNa/GVR-Coder.

</details>


### [381] [Chem World: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Physics-Informed Framework for Trustworthy Chemical Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28079)
*Tianyou Bai,Huan Wang,Mingchen Gao,Fangyue Lin,Pinze Ren,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chem World，一个整合17个化学数据集、超过80万分子样本的化学性质预测综合基准，并提出了Mixture-PINN，一种融合化学先验知识的物理信息神经网络框架，用于提升化学性质预测的准确性、鲁棒性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学性质预测基准存在任务多样性有限、数据集碎片化、评估协议不一致等问题，难以系统评估AI模型的可靠性和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建Chem World综合基准，整合17个化学数据集（超过80万分子样本），覆盖密度、电导率、溶解度等多种化学性质；2) 提出Mixture-PINN，一种物理信息神经网络框架，将化学先验知识融入数据驱动学习中。

Result: 在Chem World基准上的大量实验表明，Mixture-PINN相比现有方法在化学性质预测任务上表现出更好的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: Chem World为计算化学领域可信AI系统的开发奠定了基础，通过大规模标准化评估与物理信息学习的结合，推动了AI驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: Chemical property prediction plays a critical role in accelerating scientific discovery in chemistry, materials science, and drug development. However, existing benchmarks often suffer from limited task diversity, fragmented datasets, and inconsistent evaluation protocols, making it challenging to systematically assess the reliability and generalization of AI models. In this work, we introduce Chem World, a comprehensive benchmark for chemical property prediction that integrates 17 diverse chemical datasets with over 800,000 molecular samples, covering various properties including density, electrical conductivity, solubility, and other molecular characteristics. Chem World provides a unified platform for evaluating AI models across multiple property prediction tasks. Furthermore, we propose Mixture-PINN, a physics-informed neural network based prediction framework that incorporates chemical prior knowledge into data-driven learning, improving the accuracy, robustness, and reliability of chemical property prediction. Extensive experiments on Chem World demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach compared with existing methods. By combining large-scale standardized evaluation with physics-informed learning, Chem World establishes a foundation for developing trustworthy AI systems for computational chemistry and advancing AI-driven scientific discovery.

</details>


### [382] [From Expert Reduction to Behavioral Divergence: Tracing Numerical State through Sparse MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28097)
*Tianyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DeepSeek-V4-Flash中数学等价的专家归约顺序在稀疏MoE执行中产生可观测差异的现象，通过冻结局部MoE状态并变化聚合语义，分析了不同累加精度和归约顺序对模型行为的影响，明确了token内与跨token的边界，并提出了数值兼容性契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索数学上等价的专家归约顺序是否会导致稀疏MoE执行产生可观测差异，以及这种差异如何跨越token边界持续存在。

Method: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash中冻结局部MoE状态，仅改变聚合语义，设计了四种方案分离操作数表示与累加器精度；对720种A模式顺序和720种B模式顺序进行系统比较，并通过192条持久轨迹验证各方案的位级一致性。

Result: A模式产生10个续写盆地，B模式产生360个精确结构类和11个盆地；P32、A、B方案改变了所有原生参考路径轨迹，而C方案在路由、token序列和文本上保持位级一致；精确FP64重建可复现下游301个状态和7步预测。

Conclusion: 专家操作数转换、累加器精度和归约顺序构成稀疏MoE运行时和后端的数值兼容性契约；相同token不必然意味着相同的自回归状态，分歧可以跨越token边界存活并在后续可见。

Abstract: Mathematically equivalent expert-reduction orders can produce observably different sparse-MoE executions. We isolate this effect in native DeepSeek-V4-Flash by freezing local MoE state and varying only aggregation semantics. Four schemes separate operand representation from accumulator precision. At one layer-5 fork, 720 A-mode orders yield 10 continuation basins; 720 B-mode orders form 360 exact structural classes and 11 basins. Under one Chinese prompt, the B classes split into 202 layoffs, 113 hiring, and 45 other continuations. Maximum-L-infinity B-branch selection separates 12, 24, and 36 of 50 prompts by 8, 16, and 32 tokens. Across 192 persistent trajectories per scheme, P32, A, and B change every native-reference route trajectory, while C preserves routes, token sequences, and texts. A separate 192-trajectory C check matches native MoE, post-mHC, next-router, and LM states bitwise. For one controlled B branch, exact post-mHC endpoint reconstruction reproduces the measured downstream trajectory. At the next decode boundary, exact FP64 reconstruction of the branch's full persistent state yields agreement for 301 downstream post-mHC states, 301 persistent-state checkpoints, 301 routes, predictions, and text over seven steps, given the same naturally generated next input. These controls identify post-mHC as an intra-token boundary and full persistent state as a cross-token continuation boundary. Identical tokens need not imply identical autoregressive state: divergence can survive a token boundary and become visible later. These results make expert operand conversion, accumulator precision, and reduction order part of a numerical compatibility contract for sparse-MoE runtimes and hardware backends. They establish controlled causal possibility, not deployment incidence; C's order invariance is limited to evaluated six-term states and schedules.

</details>


### [383] [Information Bottleneck Learning for Faithful Time Series Forecasting Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28124)
*Xu Zheng,Wei Cheng,Zhuomin Chen,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IB-Forecast，一个内在可解释的多元时间序列预测框架，通过信息瓶颈约束的稀疏掩码和周期性分解，在保持与黑盒模型相当预测精度的同时提供高保真度的解释，且仅需14-20%的观测数据即可实现低误差预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释预测器虽然揭示内部结构，但无法保证结构真实反映预测依据；而保真度导向的方法几乎仅适用于事后分类任务。预测领域缺乏一个既内在可解释又能保证解释保真度的框架。

Method: IB-Forecast将预测分解为学习到的周期分量和基于可解释掩码的残差分量。通过预算约束的信息瓶颈进行端到端优化，使用户能直接控制解释稀疏度，并引入严格的保真度评估协议。

Result: 实验表明IB-Forecast在预测误差上与领先黑盒模型相当，且无需额外推断成本即可提供保真解释。在匹配稀疏度预算下，其原生解释在全部数据集上持续优于基于梯度、遮挡和优化的基线方法。

Conclusion: 现有可解释预测器的原生解释保真度较差，而IB-Forecast保证了高解释保真度，仅需14-20%的观测数据即可实现低误差预测。

Abstract: As forecasts increasingly drive decisions in fields such as energy, transportation, and healthcare, understanding the historical data behind these predictions has become as crucial as the predictions themselves. Although existing interpretable-by-design forecasters reveal their internal structures, they offer no guarantee that these structures faithfully reflect the underlying evidence driving the predictions. In contrast, while faithfulness-oriented methods explicitly verify model behavior, they are almost exclusively designed for post-hoc classification tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose IB-Forecast, an inherently interpretable multivariate time-series forecasting framework. It decomposes forecasting into a learned periodic component and a residual component computed with explainable masks over input tokens. With a budget-constrained information bottleneck, end-to-end optimization enables users to directly control explanation sparsity. With a rigorous faithfulness evaluation protocol, extensive experiments demonstrate that IB-Forecast matches the forecasting error of leading black-box models while providing faithful explanations at no additional inference cost. Furthermore, under a matched sparsity budget, these native explanations consistently surpass gradient-based, occlusion-based, and optimization-based baselines across all evaluated datasets. Ultimately, whereas the native explanations of existing interpretable forecasters exhibit poor faithfulness, IB-Forecast guarantees high explanation fidelity, requiring only 14-20% of the observations to deliver low-error predictions.

</details>


### [384] [LM-GRASP: Instance-Specific Language Models for Combinatorial Construction via Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28135)
*Mohand Mezmaz,Grégoire Danoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LM-GRASP，一种将GRASP元启发式框架的随机构造阶段重新构建为在线模仿学习任务的混合元启发式方法。它使用Transformer作为构造策略，通过行为克隆在动态精英轨迹存档上在线训练，无需离线预训练或问题特定的特征工程。在Taillard PFSP基准上优于GPU-GRASP约28.4 makespan单位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习用于组合优化的方法依赖强化学习在大型离线数据集上训练神经构造器，存在预训练成本高且泛化能力差的问题。经典的GRASP依赖静态、短视的启发式规则，缺乏数据驱动能力。

Method: 将GRASP的随机构造阶段重构为在线模仿学习任务：局部搜索过程作为专家oracle，decoder-only Transformer作为构造策略。采用迭代的"学习-推理-改进"循环，通过行为克隆在动态精英轨迹存档上在线训练策略，仅通过目标评估器与领域交互。

Result: 在Taillard PFSP基准（ta51-ta60）上，LM-GRASP比GPU-GRASP平均提升28.4 makespan单位，该提升幅度与GPU加速相对于串行执行的增益（27.2单位）相当，但标准差有重叠。

Conclusion: 实例特定、在线训练的语言模型是手工构造器的有前景且实用的替代方案，尤其适用于经典贪婪构造方法难以应对的优化景观。

Abstract: Machine learning for combinatorial optimization typically relies on neural constructors trained via reinforcement learning on large offline datasets for a fixed problem class-incurring high pretraining costs and generalizing poorly outside the training distribution. We propose an alternative: a metaheuristic framework that reformulates the randomized constructive phase of GRASP as an online imitation learning task, trained from scratch on each problem instance. A local search procedure acts as an expert oracle, while a decoder-only Transformer serves as the constructive policy. Unlike classical GRASP, which relies on static, myopic heuristic rules based on localized scalar costs, our approach is fully data-driven: the construction policy emerges from high-quality solutions discovered during the search itself, with no problem-specific feature engineering required.
  We instantiate this as LM-GRASP, a hybrid metaheuristic following an iterative learn-infer-improve cycle, training the policy online via behavioral cloning on a dynamic archive of elite trajectories-no external data or offline pretraining needed. The pipeline interfaces with the domain solely through the objective evaluator used by local search.
  Evaluated on the Taillard PFSP benchmark (ta51-ta60), the most discriminating block due to half its optima being unknown, LM-GRASP outperforms GPU-GRASP by 28.4 makespan units on average-comparable to the gain from GPU acceleration over sequential execution (27.2 units), though with overlapping standard deviations. This suggests instance-specific, online-trained language models are a promising, practical alternative to hand-engineered constructors, especially for landscapes resistant to classical greedy construction.

</details>


### [385] [Search Strategies for Optimal Classification and Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28170)
*Jacobus G. M. van der Linden,Mim van den Bos,Emir Demirović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的最优决策树（ODT）算法框架，统一了此前使用的各种搜索策略，并基于该框架实证比较了18种搜索策略，发现最优策略在分类任务上显著优于现有技术水平，在回归任务上运行时间提升超过一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优决策树模型虽然具有紧凑、可解释等优点，但可扩展性仍是难题。尽管已有多种搜索策略被提出以提升可扩展性，但各策略的具体贡献尚不明确，缺乏统一的比较视角。

Method: 提出一个通用的ODT算法框架，该框架可以实例化已有的搜索策略并支持定义新策略，为理解和比较不同策略提供统一视角，并在此框架下实证评估18种搜索策略。

Result: 在评估中，最优策略在分类任务上取得了显著更好的"任意时刻"（anytime）性能，在回归任务上运行时间比现有最优方法提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 统一框架有效帮助厘清各搜索策略的贡献，找到的最优策略显著提升了ODT的可扩展性和求解效率。

Abstract: Optimal decision trees (ODTs) are compact, interpretable machine learning models that globally optimize a given objective, but their scalability remains challenging. While recent work has proposed a variety of search strategies to improve scalability, the precise contribution of each strategy remains unclear. To address this gap, we introduce a general algorithmic framework for ODTs that instantiates previously used search strategies and enables the definition of new ones. This provides a common lens through which to understand and compare different strategies, which we use to empirically investigate the effect of 18 search strategies. Compared to the state of the art, the best strategy in our evaluation achieves significantly better anytime performance for classification, and improves runtime by more than an order of magnitude for regression.

</details>


### [386] [Multi-channel Uplift Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28182)
*Changjian Liu,Tianyu Wang,Xiaoxuan Deng,WenTao Zhu,Yuwei Xu,Jungqi Jin,Yong Gao,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReAlloc框架，解决电商平台多渠道营销预算分配中的因果决策问题，通过快速-慢速双网络（Orthogonal Teacher与Explanation-Guided Student）在单纯形约束下进行支持感知的保守预算分配，并在淘宝大规模在线实验中获得支付订单和收入的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台需要在多个渠道间分配固定营销预算以最大化商业效用，但标准的预测-优化（PTO）范式在组合空间中因观测混杂和严重外推而失效，需要新的方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出ReAlloc快速-慢速因果框架：敏捷的Orthogonal Teacher从短期日志中提取无偏局部梯度，Explanation-Guided Student将梯度蒸馏为长期时间跨度上的结构化边际场，实现支持感知的保守决策，捕捉跨渠道替代效应，并采用单纯形约束的uplift决策公式。

Result: 在大量模拟实验和淘宝平台的大规模在线A/B测试中，ReAlloc在支付订单和收入两个指标上均实现了同步提升。

Conclusion: ReAlloc成功解决了组合空间中预算分配因观测混杂和严重外推导致的失效问题，验证了快速-慢速因果框架在真实电商场景中的有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce platforms must allocate fixed marketing budgets across multiple channels to maximize business utility. However, standard predict-then-optimize (PTO) paradigms fail in this compositional space due to observational confounding and severe extrapolation. We formulate this challenge as a simplex-constrained uplift decision problem and propose ReAlloc, a fast-slow causal framework. Specifically, an agile Orthogonal Teacher extracts unbiased local gradients from short-term logs, while an Explanation-Guided Student distills them into a structured marginal field over long-term horizons. This design enables support-aware, conservative decisions that capture cross-channel substitutions. Extensive simulations and large-scale online A/B tests on Taobao platform demonstrate that ReAlloc achieves simultaneous lifts in both pay order and income.

</details>


### [387] [Persistent Gaussian Perturbations Prevent Oversmoothing in Recurrent Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28185)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了向循环图神经网络（GNN）每个传播步骤后注入高斯噪声的架构，将其建模为随机动力系统，证明了在全局收缩假设下，隐藏表示形成几何遍历马尔可夫链，且稳态Dirichlet能量存在正下界（与噪声方差和图的谱间隙成正比），从而严格保证渐进过平滑被防止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过平滑是深度图神经网络的根本性限制，重复消息传递导致节点表示趋于相似并坍缩到低维子空间，限制了消息传递架构的有效深度，因此需要寻找保持表示多样性的机制。

Method: 将带有逐层独立高斯噪声注入的循环GNN建模为随机动力系统，在确定性更新的全局收缩假设下，利用马尔可夫链理论证明隐藏表示的几何遍历性及唯一不变概率测度的存在性，并通过理论推导给出期望稳态Dirichlet能量的显式正下界。

Result: 证明了稳态表示的预期Dirichlet能量下界与噪声方差和图谱间隙成正比，稳态表示不会坍缩到常数流形；在线性和非线性循环GNN上的数值实验与理论预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 持续随机扰动是一种与残差连接、归一化、图重连等确定性方法根本不同的抗过平滑机制，为深度GNN的设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a fundamental limitation of deep graph neural networks (GNNs), where repeated message passing causes node representations to become increasingly similar, eventually collapsing toward a low-dimensional subspace. This phenomenon limits the effective depth of message-passing architectures and motivates the search for mechanisms that preserve representation diversity. In this paper, we study a recurrent graph neural network in which independent Gaussian noise is injected after every propagation step and analyze the resulting architecture as a stochastic dynamical system. Under a standard global contraction assumption on the deterministic update, we prove that the hidden representations form a geometrically ergodic Markov chain admitting a unique invariant probability measure. Our main theoretical result establishes an explicit positive lower bound on the expected stationary Dirichlet energy, proportional to both the noise variance and the spectral gap of the underlying graph. Consequently, the stationary representations cannot collapse onto the constant manifold, providing a rigorous guarantee that asymptotic oversmoothing is prevented in the sense of non-vanishing Dirichlet energy. Our analysis reveals persistent stochastic perturbations as a fundamentally different mechanism for combating oversmoothing, complementing existing deterministic approaches based on residual connections, normalization, and graph rewiring. Finally, numerical experiments on both linear and nonlinear recurrent graph neural networks closely match the theoretical predictions, illustrating the emergence of a stationary distribution and the predicted dependence of the limiting Dirichlet energy on the noise intensity.

</details>


### [388] [TopoFormer: Topology Meets Attention for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28259)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Astrit Tola,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Topoformer，一种轻量级且可扩展的图表示学习框架，通过Topo-Scan模块将图分解为有序的拓扑令牌序列，并利用Transformer进行处理，实现多尺度结构模式捕捉与图级嵌入生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持久同调（persistent homology）方法计算代价高昂且难以并行化，而现有图神经网络（GNN）在捕捉全局拓扑结构方面存在不足。作者旨在开发一种既能编码拓扑结构、又能无缝集成深度学习架构的高效图表示学习方法。

Method: 提出Topoformer框架，核心是Topo-Scan模块：通过对节点或边过滤（filtration）进行切片，将图分解为短而有序的拓扑令牌序列，捕捉从局部模体到全局组织的多尺度结构模式，再由Transformer处理生成图级嵌入。该方法完全可并行化，避免了传统持久同调中昂贵的图表计算。

Result: 在图分类和分子性质预测基准上取得了最先进的性能。Topoformer在匹配或超越强GNN和基于拓扑的基线方法的同时，还提供了可预测且高效的计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 本文为图表示学习开辟了新的路径，展示了将拓扑归纳偏置集成到注意力框架中的统一且可并行化的方法，具有理论稳定性保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce Topoformer, a lightweight and scalable framework for graph representation learning that encodes topological structure into attention-friendly sequences. At the core of our method is Topo-Scan, a novel module that decomposes a graph into a short, ordered sequence of topological tokens by slicing over node or edge filtrations. These sequences capture multi-scale structural patterns, from local motifs to global organization, and are processed by a Transformer to produce expressive graph-level embeddings. Unlike traditional persistent homology pipelines, Topo-Scan is parallelizable, avoids costly diagram computations, and integrates seamlessly with standard deep learning architectures. We provide theoretical guarantees on the stability of our topological encodings and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across graph classification and molecular property prediction benchmarks. Our results show that Topoformer matches or exceeds strong GNN and topology-based baselines while offering predictable and efficient compute. This work opens a new path for parallelizable and unifying approaches to graph representation learning that integrate topological inductive biases into attention frameworks.

</details>


### [389] [HARGO: Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization for RL Post-Training of LLMs on HPC Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28301)
*Tiangang Li,Xiangbo Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARGO（异构性感知奖励引导优化）方法，通过置信度调制的优势加权，在不依赖任务类型标签的情况下，解决HPC任务中监督微调后模型行为不精确以及统一权重RL方法（如GRPO）在异构任务上表现次优的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调虽然能为LLM装备HPC领域知识，但知识不等于正确行为——同一模型在数据竞争检测上表现良好，却在事实问答中产生冗长不精确的答案。HPC任务间存在极大的异构性（答案长度相差58倍、三种不同奖励分布、SFT准确率差异大），使得统一权重的RL方法（如GRPO）效果次优。

Method: HARGO通过两个信号实现每个响应的重要性加权：1）从组级奖励对比中计算判别信号；2）从参考模型对数概率中计算置信度信号。两者调制优势后再计算逐响应权重，整个过程不需要任务类型标签。

Result: 在四个HPC任务和九种方法的对比中，HARGO在三个主要指标上均取得最佳表现：WinRate 54.62%，Data Race F1 91.30%，PLP相似度0.8558。消融实验证实两个信号具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: HARGO在异构HPC任务中建立了最佳的整体对齐质量，验证了异构性感知的RL后训练方法对HPC场景的有效性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can equip large language models (LLMs) with domain knowledge for high-performance computing (HPC) tasks such as data race detection and benchmark question answering. However, knowledge alone does not guarantee task-appropriate behavior: the same SFT model that correctly classifies 88.65\% of C/C++ data race samples produces verbose, imprecise answers to factual queries, with 65.9\% of MLPerf responses exceeding 40 characters. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training addresses this gap by optimizing for task-specific rewards rather than token-level imitation. Yet HPC tasks exhibit extreme heterogeneity, with binary classification, factual QA, and semantic generation differing by 58x in answer length, spanning three distinct reward distributions, and showing widely varying SFT accuracy. This makes uniform-weight RL methods such as GRPO suboptimal. We propose HARGO, Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization, which introduces per-response importance weighting via confidence-modulated advantage: computing a discrimination signal from group-level reward contrast and a confidence signal from reference model log-probabilities, then modulating the advantage before computing per-response weights, without requiring task-type labels. Across four HPC tasks and nine methods, HARGO achieves the best performance on all three primary metrics: WinRate 54.62\%, Data Race F1 91.30\%, and PLP Similarity 0.8558. Ablation confirms complementary contributions from both signals. HARGO establishes the best overall alignment quality among compared methods for heterogeneous HPC tasks.

</details>


### [390] [Semi-Supervised Learning for Molecular Graphs via Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28304)
*Rasmus Tirsgaard,Laurits Fredsgaard,Marisa Wodrich,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用集成共识的半监督学习方法，在分子科学领域无需标签保持增强即可提升图神经网络在分子属性预测等任务上的准确率、鲁棒性和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中标注数据获取成本高，而大量未标注分子数据容易获得。传统的半监督学习方法依赖标签保持的数据增强，但在分子领域微小变化可能大幅改变属性，难以设计有效的增强策略。

Method: 采用基于集成共识（ensemble consensus）的半监督学习目标，无需设计标签保持增强，在多种分子数据集、任务类型和图神经网络架构上进行训练。

Result: 集成共识训练显著提升预测精度，效果类似于知识蒸馏；单个集成成员在几乎所有情况下优于传统监督方式训练的全集成，同时降低了校准误差。

Conclusion: 集成共识半监督学习是分子领域有效的替代方案，克服了传统半监督方法在分子数据增强上的困难，并带来精度、鲁棒性和校准的多重提升。

Abstract: Machine learning is transforming molecular sciences by accelerating property prediction, simulation, and the discovery of new molecules and materials. Acquiring labeled data in these domains is often costly and time-consuming, whereas large collections of unlabeled molecular data are readily available. Standard semi-supervised learning methods often rely on label-preserving augmentations, which are challenging to design in the molecular domain, where minor changes can drastically alter properties. In this work, we show that semi-supervised methods that rely on an ensemble consensus can boost predictive accuracy across a diverse range of molecular datasets, task types, and graph neural network architectures. We find that training with an ensemble consensus objective increases robustness in models and exhibits an effect similar to knowledge distillation; an individual member of an ensemble trained this way outperforms a full ensemble trained in a traditional supervised fashion in almost all cases. In addition, this type of semi-supervised training reduces calibration error.

</details>


### [391] [Beyond Geometric Complementarity: Coherent Overlap in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28308)
*Huiyuan Tian,Bonan Xu,Shijian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏混合专家（MoE）语言模型中专家共选与表示方向的关系，提出了专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）等新指标，区分了路由一致性、候选质量和候选-上下文交互，发现了一个称为"相干重叠"的模式：专家子空间高度重叠但路由选择仍能解释更多残差表示，且几何上的重叠并不意味着功能冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常混淆路由一致性、候选质量和候选-上下文交互这三个量，导致对MoE模型中专家共选带来几何效益的理解存在偏差。作者希望厘清这些概念，回答"共同被选中的专家是否贡献不同的表示方向"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出了三个分析工具：专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）用于量化专家子空间重叠程度；匹配路由残差用于对比实际路由与替代路由的表示解释能力；前缀控制的2×2因子设计用于分离候选与上下文交互。同时使用冻结路由干预和有对照的Top-k训练研究来评估功能价值。

Result: 在六个MoE架构中，专家子空间高度重叠但实际路由比匹配替代方案更好地解释token表示。在39个因子单元格中，所选候选在所有单元格中都比最强的未选中竞争对手解释更多残差，但实际前缀缩小了这一优势（所有交互均为负，95%置信区间均低于零）。在39个冻结路由比较中，增加后续专家在24个比较中改善了下一token预测，其余15个不明确；受控训练研究在三个种子中均支持Top-2优于Top-1。

Conclusion: MoE路由从共享的几何邻域中选择与token相关的专家，而有用的多专家计算在没有不相交的线性覆盖的情况下仍然存在。几何相似性本身不能单独决定冗余性或剪枝价值，这为理解MoE模型的工作机制和模型压缩提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models route each token to multiple experts, suggesting a geometric account of their benefit: co-selected experts should contribute distinct representation directions. Existing evidence often conflates route coherence, candidate quality, and candidate-by-context interaction. We distinguish these quantities using an Expert Subspace Separation Index (ESSI), matched-route residuals, and a prefix-controlled $2\times2$ factorial; frozen-route interventions and a controlled Top-$k$ study assess functional value. Three paired contrasts organize the findings. First, across six MoE architectures, expert subspaces overlap substantially, yet actual routes explain token representations better than matched alternatives. Second, across the 39 factorial cells in OLMoE, Mixtral, and DeepSeek, the selected candidate explains more of the residual representation than the strongest unselected rival in every cell, yet the actual prefix narrows this advantage throughout: all interactions are negative, and every 95% confidence interval lies below zero. Third, this geometric narrowing does not imply functional redundancy: adding later experts improves next-token prediction in 24 of 39 frozen-route comparisons, while the other 15 estimates are inconclusive; a controlled training study also favors Top-2 over Top-1 in all three seeds. We call this joint pattern coherent overlap: routing selects token-relevant experts from a shared geometric neighborhood, while useful multi-expert computation persists without disjoint linear coverage. Separating these quantities clarifies why geometric similarity alone cannot determine redundancy or pruning value.

</details>


### [392] [Measuring Distortion in the Empty Regions of Dimensionality Reduction Scatterplots with the Gap Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28324)
*Jaume Ros,Alessio Arleo,Fernando Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gap Index (GI)，一种用于衡量二维降维投影中空白区域视觉失真的质量指标，通过将空间分解为三角形并比较其高维对应物来计算变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的质量指标主要关注点与点之间的直接关系（如距离或邻域），忽略了投影布局中空白区域的失真，而这些空白区域往往构成2D布局中视觉相关的特征。

Method: 将投影空间分解为空白三角形，将其与高维空间中的对应三角形进行比较，计算每个三角形的变形程度；变形可以聚合为单个标量值，也可以叠加在投影上可视化区域失真模式。

Result: 与流行的质量指标相反，GI 对具有高视觉影响的小型结构变形敏感；计算速度快且结果可解释。

Conclusion: Gap Index 是一种快速、可解释且对视觉上显著的结构变形敏感的新型质量指标，弥补了现有指标忽略空白区域失真的不足。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [393] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAMECHE方法，将基于元数据的聚类联邦学习重构为分布式期望最大化过程，仅限制服务器进行加法操作，从而兼容安全的联邦学习加密方案，在隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类联邦学习（CFL）面临隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间的权衡（即CFL三难困境）。现有基于元数据的聚类方法虽然通信和计算高效，但不兼容标准的联邦学习隐私保护机制。

Method: 将基于元数据的CFL形式化为分布式期望最大化（EM）过程，限制服务器更新仅限于加法操作，从而实现与实用安全联邦学习方案的兼容，同时保持聚类效率。

Result: 在多个数据集和各种异质性场景下的广泛实验表明，FLAMECHE提升了客户端模型的有效性，并实现了加密兼容的基于元数据的聚类，改善了其在CFL三难困境中的位置。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE通过将元数据聚类的服务器操作限制为加法，成功实现了与安全联邦学习方案的兼容，在CFL三难困境中取得了更优的平衡点。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [394] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerMind框架，通过结构化的证据账本约束多模态智能体的推理轨迹，确保所有推理步骤都有工具产出的来源支撑，并设计了三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器和事件触发验证修复引擎，在提升答案准确率的同时增强了轨迹级忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视觉问答智能体的评估仅关注最终答案准确率，无法判断正确答案是来源于真实的接地证据、语言先验还是误差抵消。这种聚合信号掩盖了推理过程中的系统性问题（如幻觉、过度推理、修复时的信息放大等）。

Method: 1) 将智能体轨迹建模为受来源约束的状态机，工具输出归一化为结构化证据账本作为轨迹状态；2) 下游推理只能引用活跃账本条目，在实体和数值层面检查接地性；3) 修复通过类型化状态转换实现，且不能引入无工具来源的内容；4) 提出三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器和事件触发验证修复引擎（带形式化的来源非放大保证）。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和多种骨干MLLM上的实验表明，LedgerMind同时提升了最终答案准确率和轨迹级忠实度，有效针对了四种被最终答案准确率掩盖的失败模式：无支撑的中间推理、引用型实体幻觉、简单查询上的过度推理以及修复期信息放大。

Conclusion: 将来源约束引入多模态智能体轨迹是提升推理可信度和可验证性的有效途径，相比仅优化最终答案准确率，关注轨迹级忠实度能够更好地暴露并修复深层推理缺陷。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [395] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家估计方法，解决平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中评论家偏差与优化成本之间的权衡问题，并据此开发了原始-对偶自然Actor-Critic算法，首次在无限时域平均奖励CMDP中实现了阶最优的收敛保证（最优性差距和约束违反均为Õ(T^{-1/2})），且适用于一般策略参数化和神经评论家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然线性评论家的原始-对偶Actor-Critic方法已被充分研究，但将阶最优收敛保证扩展到神经评论家的平均奖励CMDP仍然是一个开放问题。主要挑战在于神经评论家估计中存在根本性的偏差-成本权衡：在神经正切核（NTK）分析下，减少评论家偏差会大幅增加评论家优化成本，从而阻碍原始-对偶框架中阶最优收敛的实现。

Method: 引入一种分层多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家，在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个层面同时进行去偏。该估计器仅以对数期望样本成本即可获得长评论家优化运行的偏差。在此基础上，构建了原始-对偶自然Actor-Critic算法。

Result: 所提出的算法实现了最优性差距和约束违反均为Õ(T^{-1/2})的阶最优收敛保证。这是首次在无限时域平均奖励CMDP中，针对一般策略参数化和神经评论家，建立阶最优收敛保证，且无需预先知道底层混合时间。该结果即使在不带约束的设置中也是新颖的。

Conclusion: 通过MLMC神经评论家设计解决了CMDP中原有的偏差-成本权衡难题，证明了神经评论家也能在安全关键强化学习场景中实现阶最优收敛，拓展了理论保证的适用范围。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [396] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT），通过在空闲屏幕期间预构建条件策略树来消除计算机使用代理在决策关键路径上的自回归解码延迟，将事件窗口内的成功率从0.50提升到0.79，且不产生错误动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理经常在瞬态GUI事件上失败，因为它们在相关窗口关闭后才产生正确动作。根本原因是昂贵的自回归解码处于决策时间的关键路径上，导致响应延迟。

Method: AAPT在空闲屏幕期间，利用相同的冻结多模态模型构建一个有界条件策略树，包含可观察的守卫条件、预授权动作和分支特定的截止时间。树的大小覆盖模型自身的解码延迟。当事件发生时，一个轻量级观察器将变化门控帧与准备好的分支匹配，立即执行相应动作而无需生成新文本。

Result: 在预设终点和精确McNemar检验的配对试验中，AAPT将成功率从0.50提高到0.79（p=1.8×10^-3），且无错误动作。开放循环和预测重规划基线均因仍在执行时解码而成功率为零。在独立通用多模态模型上126次配对试验中复现效果（p=4.9×10^-13）。消融实验揭示三个关键需求：快速观察器解码、有效树规划和准确分支路由。

Conclusion: AAPT在可预先枚举候选动作的场景中表现最佳，而反应式执行在无法预先枚举动作时仍然更强。预注册的oracle探针拒绝了初始假设，指出分支路由是因果瓶颈。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [397] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模动作空间下上下文赌博机中的策略学习问题，涵盖在线学习和离线学习两种范式，提出了多种结构化贝叶斯方法和悲观学习方法来解决探索效率低、数据覆盖稀疏、方差高等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模动作空间中，上下文赌博机的在线和离线学习面临探索效率低下、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重方差高、外推偏差以及优化困难等重大挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 本文提出两类方法：(1) 在线学习方面，提出混合效应Thompson采样（meTS）和基于扩散启发先验的dTS方法，利用结构化先验在动作间共享信息；(2) 离线学习方面，提出基于潜变量的结构化直接方法（sDM）、可高效优化的凹策略加权对数似然目标，以及基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayes界的可微分悲观方法。

Result: 所提出的方法在遗憾界方面依赖于有效动作数量而非全部动作数量，表明方法能够有效利用动作间的结构信息；同时揭示了在大动作空间中优化误差可能主导估计误差这一重要现象。

Conclusion: 本文通过结构化贝叶斯方法和悲观学习策略，系统地解决了大规模动作空间上下文赌博机中在线与离线策略学习的核心难题，为高维动作空间下的决策问题提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [398] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu,Xiaoyu Hu,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QQWorld，用分位数-分位数（QQ）匹配目标替代Epps-Pulley（EP）正则化，改善潜在世界模型中重尾潜在分布的控制，提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel使用Epps-Pulley目标将潜在变量正则化为各向同性高斯分布，但EP的修正梯度在孤立尾部样本上迅速消失，导致重尾偏差无法被有效控制，影响潜在世界模型的规划性能。

Method: 提出QQWorld，用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代EP目标，直接将投影后的潜在样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数对齐，从而在尾部保持有效修正梯度。进一步提出跨批次QQ方法，利用先前批次的分离样本扩大有效排序池，并分析其偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld有效提升了LeWM的平均规划成功率，同时持续获得更好的高斯对齐效果和更薄的潜在尾部。

Conclusion: QQ匹配目标相比EP正则化能更有效地控制潜在分布的尾部偏差，提升潜在世界模型的规划性能，且跨批次QQ在扩大排序池与偏差-方差之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>


### [399] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAdapt是一个面向表面码量子纠错的噪声自适应神经预解码框架，通过捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应不断演化的噪声条件，同时缓解灾难性遗忘，并将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。在110种合成分布外噪声配置和Google Willow基准数据上均显著降低了逻辑错误率和后端解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算依赖量子纠错抑制物理错误，但性能受限于经典解码器处理快速生成的综合征数据的延迟。硬件噪声具有强、异质、非平稳的特点，且模拟到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能，因此需要能够适应演化噪声条件的解码框架。

Method: 提出QAdapt噪声自适应神经预解码框架，包含三个关键组件：捕获综合征数据中局部时空相关性的神经网络；顺序适应演化噪声条件并缓解灾难性遗忘的适应机制；将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器的兼容性设计。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置中，QAdapt一致地降低了相对于神经预解码基线的逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上，无需目标域微调即可实现逻辑错误率最高降低5.79%，后端解码延迟最高降低9.32%。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，能够提升量子纠错在演化硬件噪声条件下的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [400] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入（KSSE）模型，利用物理学启发的能量模型替代密集CNN分类器，通过稀疏图谱嵌入在ImageNet-1000上以约21M参数达到88.93%的Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L并匹配ViT-H，模型规模分别缩小10倍和30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集CNN分类器参数规模庞大且计算开销高，作者希望利用物理学中的谱嵌入和稀疏图结构，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低模型参数量和计算复杂度。

Method: 将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构建正则化拉普拉斯算子作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量，通过FFT在循环块上求解D个独立通道谱问题（O(N log N + k²N)），使用star-domain surgery优化图拓扑，并利用多尺度分形分析验证损失景观从粗糙到星域盆地的转变，结合Rayleigh refinement实现高效特征分类。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000转导协议下，使用冻结的EfficientNet-B4特征（D=1792），KSSE以约21.24M参数达到88.93% Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L（197M, 86.4-87.3%），匹配ViT-H/14（632M, 88.0-89.5%），模型规模分别缩小10倍和30倍。论文还证明了六个理论结果。

Conclusion: KSSE证明了物理学启发的谱嵌入方法可以在极低参数量的情况下达到甚至超越大规模Transformer模型的分类性能，为高效图像分类提供了一条新的路径，同时通过六个理论结果保证了方法的数学严谨性。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [401] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"短时图记忆"的即插即用模块，通过在线图神经网络代理预筛选候选分子，在固定oracle预算下优先评估预测效用更高的分子，从而提升分子优化的top-10得分，且不增加oracle调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，决定评估什么与决定生成什么同样重要。现有方法专注于改进分子生成器本身，却忽略了如何更有效地分配oracle查询预算。

Method: 提出短时图记忆（short-term graph memory）模块，作为即插即用组件：保留生成器原有架构和更新规则，通过在线图神经网络代理对每轮候选池进行预筛选，将有限的oracle预算优先分配给预测效用更高的分子。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准上，基于片段生成器应用该模块后，平均top-10得分提升且不增加oracle成本；在1000次调用的严格预算下，所有四种测试生成器均有收益。通过分析代理引导选择与生成器探索/利用行为的交互，发现其收益与骨干网络的搜索广度和原生搜索对oracle反馈的利用效率一致。

Conclusion: 提供了一种更有效利用固定oracle预算的简单方法，并揭示了哪些生成器能从中获益及其原因，为分子优化中的预算分配策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [402] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合思维链推理、自动提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励强化学习的告警分类器，用于解决安全运营中心（SOC）中的告警疲劳问题，达到82.6%的测试准确率，并显著优于直接标签的LLM分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC面临告警疲劳问题，检测数量超过人员处理能力。现有方法直接提示或微调LLM输出分类标签，但没有训练模型对检测是否为真实威胁进行推理。

Method: 在真实的人工标注Windows端点检测数据上训练思维链推理的分类器，结合自动提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励的强化学习；同时单独训练一个校准器来读取完整推理轨迹并估计判决正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%测试准确率；在高置信度操作点（自动分流控制点）下，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%；经过训练的校准器是必要的（未训练的置信度判断器会将高置信度召回率降为零）；微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。

Conclusion: 目标化的针对性训练优于依赖更大规模的通用模型，思维链推理结合专门的校准器能有效提升安全告警分类的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [403] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果performative框架的算法追索方法，通过结构因果模型建模追索行为对真实标签的影响，解决了传统追索方法忽略行为响应导致性能退化的问题，并在信用数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法仅关注翻转模型预测，未考虑推荐变化是否能真实提升个体资质或只是策略性欺骗分类器，导致追索策略在模型重训练后失效且预测精度下降。

Method: 提出因果performative框架，通过结构因果模型建模追索行为在特征间的传播及其对真实标签的影响；将问题形式化为非凸优化，并刻画performatively稳定解存在及可高效计算的条件，通过简单迭代动力学求解。

Result: 在半合成和真实信用数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多轮模型重训练场景下优于标准经验风险最小化，显著降低了策略性行为导致的分步偏移和博弈动机。

Conclusion: 因果感知的追索策略能够达到稳定均衡，减少博弈行为，降低对反复模型重训练的需求，而忽视因果结构的追索策略会引发大规模误导性行为响应。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [404] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同一图结构上节点分类（NC）和链接预测（LP）之间的跨任务迁移问题，提出了无泄漏的评估协议，发现NC→LP在同质性图上稳定有益，而LP→NC仅在特定条件下有效，并提出了CoTask Score（CTS）指标来总结联合任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在评估同一图上NC和LP的跨任务迁移时，常使用不兼容的数据划分、可观测图假设和负采样规则，导致关于跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。作者希望建立严格的协议来正确评估这种迁移。

Method: 提出无泄漏的NC-LP迁移评估协议：固定节点和边划分、使用排除评估边的共享消息传递图、对LP使用固定负样本。在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上进行实验，并提出CTS指标用于衡量共享编码器在NC和LP联合任务上的效用。

Result: 发现迁移具有强烈的方向性：NC→LP在同质性图上一致有益；LP→NC在朴素表征重用下表现脆弱甚至降低精度，仅在结构主导（LP容易但NC未饱和）的机制下可靠正向。数据集统计量（尤其是同质性）可指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: 同一图上的NC和LP跨任务迁移存在方向性差异，LP可被视为结构预训练但条件严苛；同质性等简单数据统计量可用于预测迁移效果，CTS可作为联合任务效用的摘要指标。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [405] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将普通在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）推广为带有可调KL正则化参数β的通用框架，通过混合参考策略与特权教师的token级logits实现廉价蒸馏，在数学推理基准上优于vanilla OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vanilla OPSD在实践中难以稳定工作，需要大量工程调优。作者发现OPSD本质上是β=1的策略优化家族特例，β被隐式固定为1，导致缺乏可控的正则化参数来平衡参考策略和教师指导之间的关系。

Method: 将β作为可调正则化参数引入OPSD，推导出最优策略为参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值；不直接优化高成本高方差的RL目标，而是将闭式解转化为蒸馏目标——通过混合两者token级logits来高效实现；同时采用return-to-go信用分配对齐token更新与序列级目标。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，β-OPSD一致优于vanilla OPSD，改善了优化稳定性和下游推理性能。

Conclusion: β-OPSD提供了一条从自蒸馏到策略优化再返回的原则性路径，在不牺牲OPSD实用效率的前提下提升推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [406] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAISEN，一个五阶段审计管线（亚组分层、差异度量、机制诊断、事后缓解、漂移监测），并在16个合成疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴上进行到失败点的评估，揭示了审计组件在关键条件下可能失效的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在总体表现良好但亚组误差率存在差异，现有审计流程的组件缺乏压力测试，导致审计结果的可靠性不明。

Method: 构建KAISEN五阶段审计管线，在合成基准（16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴、3个预设交叉）上进行系统性评估，检验显著性跟踪、阈值优化、机制诊断、CUSUM漂移监测等组件的失效条件。

Result: 四项主要发现：(i) 显著性跟踪与各轴最小可检测效应相关（标准化后rho从0.56升至0.78）；(ii) 阈值优化在48/48次运行中降低EOD，而Platt缩放仅19/48次改善且均值接近零；(iii) 机制诊断在144/144受控案例中正确，但在代理误设下48个模型驱动案例全部未检出且无失败信号；(iv) CUSUM的假警报和漏检主要取决于队列种子而非疾病（p=0.002），阈值无法跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: 审计管线组件在特定条件下会失效且无失败提示信号，审计报告应关注方差而非平均值；所有结果基于合成数据，不构成临床有效性证据。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [407] [Auditing Emergent LLM-Agent Collaboration through Cooperation-Obligation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27429)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Hanqing Yang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出iCORE框架，通过统一编码$(G,Q,Π)$（合作图、义务图、审计映射）来审计LLM多智能体系统的协作过程，实现工作健全性和智能体分配稳定性两个可认证属性，并在实验中显著提升轨迹质量和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统通过动态自组织和涌现协作解决复杂任务，但现有审计方法无法同时表示"什么工作未完成、谁负责、以及每个工作状态转换的证据"，存在审计缺口。

Method: 提出iCORE（Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation），构建统一编码$X=(G,Q,Π)$：G为合作图（可观测交互），Q为义务图（演化中的工作和分配），Π为连接两者的审计映射（含可验证属性和证据）。基于此建立工作健全性和智能体分配稳定性的认证机制，并作为工作流上的仪器化层。

Result: 建立了局部到全局的健全性保证、分配遗憾保证和性能界限。数值结果表明，iCORE能精确重构两种执行模式下的健全性和分配缺陷；相比被动全状态观测，iCORE-Audit在受控和真实LLM执行中分别提升绝对轨迹质量11.5%和26.4%，绝对终端性能提升15.1%和31.0%。

Conclusion: iCORE作为一种轻量级仪器化层，能够有效弥合LLM多智能体系统的审计缺口，通过统一表示工作状态、责任归属和证据链来提升系统透明度和响应质量。

Abstract: LLM-agent systems can solve complex tasks through dynamic self-organization and emergent cooperation. Auditing this process is essential because plausible intermediate or final outputs can conceal incomplete or unsupported work and poorly allocated responsibility, ultimately compromising response quality. While existing approaches may record messages, tool calls, provenance, or task dependencies, an auditability gap exists as they do not jointly represent what work remains, who is responsible for it, and what evidence justifies each work-state transition. We address this auditability gap by proposing \emph{Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation} (iCORE). It creates a unified encoding $X=(G,Q,Π)$ integrating observable interactions as a cooperation graph $G$, evolving work and assignments as an obligation graph $Q$, and the audit map $Π$ linking them with verifiable properties and evidence. This iCORE representation enables the auditor to certify two complementary properties: {Work soundness}, where every active decision-relevant work assertion must have a finite justification through $G$ and $Π$; and {Agent-assignment stability}, which requires that no feasible alternative agent improve the declared contribution value for an evaluated obligation by more than $ε$. We establish local-to-global soundness and assignment-regret guarantees and a performance bound under stated conditions. iCORE is an instrumentation layer over workflows. Numerical results show that the full coupled state exactly reconstructs soundness and assignment defects in two execution modes and that, relative to passive full-state observation, iCORE-Audit yields absolute trajectory-quality improvements of $11.5\%$ and $26.4\%$ in controlled and real-LLM execution, respectively, with corresponding absolute terminal-performance improvements of $15.1\%$ and $31.0\%$.

</details>


### [408] [Stop Shipping AI Agents on Faith: Capability Is Not Production Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27677)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ProofAgent Index (PAI)，一个用于AI智能体生产就绪度评估的治理指数，将部署就绪从能力导向重新定义为基于评估、上下文、合规和治理四个维度的可审计决策，并在医疗和金融领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已进入生产工作流，但许多发布决策仍依赖能力信号、演示或行为测试，无法证明智能体在生产约束下的就绪度。能力不等于生产就绪，需要一个更全面的治理就绪评估框架。

Method: 提出ProofAgent Index (PAI)，结合四个维度的部署证据：评估（观测行为）、上下文（影响行为的运行环境）、合规（与适用规则和控制的对齐）、治理（组织授权、监控、审计和控制能力）。PAI在开源ProofAgent Harness中实现，支持可审计的AI智能体评估和治理。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个高度监管领域的验证表明，PAI携带了保留的就绪信号，能区分高风险和低风险配置。研究发现上下文工程显著改变可靠性，能力改善行为但不能决定就绪度，治理证据必须保持可见而非被平均化抹去。

Conclusion: PAI将智能体发布从基于信任的部署决策重构为可审计的就绪度决策，为AI智能体的生产部署提供了更严谨的治理评估方法。

Abstract: AI agents are moving into production workflows where they retrieve information, call tools, maintain state, and act on behalf of users or organizations, but many release decisions still rely on capability signals, demos, or behavioral tests that do not show whether an agent is ready to operate under production constraints. Capability is therefore not production readiness. This paper introduces the ProofAgent Index (PAI), a governance readiness index for AI agents. PAI combines four dimensions of deployment evidence: Evaluation, Context, Compliance, and Governance. Evaluation measures observed behavior, Context measures the operating environment that shapes that behavior, Compliance measures alignment with applicable rules and controls, and Governance measures whether the organization can authorize, monitor, audit, and control the agent during operation. PAI is implemented inside ProofAgent Harness, an open source infrastructure for auditable AI agent evaluation and governance. Validation across two heavily regulated domains, healthcare and finance, shows that PAI carries held out readiness signal and separates higher risk from lower risk configurations. The results show that context engineering strongly changes reliability, capability improves behavior but does not determine readiness, and governance evidence must remain visible rather than averaged away. PAI reframes agent release from a faith based deployment decision into an auditable readiness decision.

</details>


### [409] [Scaling LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Systems: Design Principles and Architectural Scalability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27942)
*Linus Sander,Fengjunjie Pan,Vahid Zolfaghari,Andre Schamschurko,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的可扩展架构设计缺乏系统化原则的问题，提炼了四条设计原则，提出了一种参考架构，并通过标准基准测试评估了不同配置的可扩展性，发现性能仅在底层LLM超过能力阈值时随规模提升，且一致性问题是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统具有实现集体智能和解决复杂任务的潜力，但其架构设计空间缺乏系统化整理和公认的设计原则，可扩展性特征也尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过结构化分析已有工作提炼四条设计原则（简洁性、弹性反馈、带可选循环的顺序工作流、基于总结的通信）；将这些原则操作化为参考架构，拓扑形式化为有约束的有向工作流图；在标准终端系统工程项目基准上用两种不同能力的LLM评估四种复杂度递增的配置。

Result: 缩放带来了可测量的精度提升且成本近似线性增长，但仅在底层LLM超过最低能力阈值时成立；性能在中等复杂度配置时达到峰值，随后因超时和评估限制而下降；一致性问题在所有规模级别上持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究为实践者提供了具体的设计指导，并指出一致性和评估标准化是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have the potential to enable collective intelligence and scale toward solving highly complex tasks through coordinated ensembles of specialized agents. However, despite their theoretical potential, the architectural design space remains largely non-systematized and lacks broadly established design principles. Furthermore, the scalability characteristics of such systems are only partially understood so far. This paper makes two contributions. We first distill four design principles for scalable MAS architectures from a structured analysis of prior work: simplicity, elastic feedback, sequential workflows with optional loops, and summary-based communication. We operationalize these principles in a reference architecture whose topology is formalized as a constrained directed workflow graph, and we evaluate four configurations of increasing complexity on a standardized benchmark of terminal-based system engineering tasks using two LLMs of differing capability. Our findings show that scaling yields measurable accuracy improvements with approximately linear cost growth, but only when the underlying LLM exceeds a minimum capability threshold. Performance peaks at intermediate complexity, then degrades due to timeouts and evaluation limitations. In addition, persistent consistency issues emerge as a central challenge across all scaling levels. These results provide concrete design guidance for practitioners and highlight consistency and evaluation standardization as key targets for future research.

</details>


### [410] [Argonaut: Interactive Visual Exploration for Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27946)
*Srijoni Majumdar,Chuhao Qin,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Argonaut是一个轻量级的容器化优化仪表盘，用于分布式离散选择优化的交互式可视化探索，支持用户上传数据集、构建代理和选项、动态修改决策空间，并运行多种算法后端来检查局部代理决策和全局目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式离散选择优化方法大多是集中式的，且只能可视化最终解或提供固定数据集上的算法后端，整个搜索过程是不透明的黑盒，难以在规模化系统中探索和导航。

Method: 构建了Argonaut，一个基于Node.js的交互式优化仪表盘，集成了可扩展的Java和Python优化后端，支持用户上传数据集、构建代理和选项、动态修改决策空间和参数，并以人机交互循环的方式运行多个算法后端进行可视化探索。

Result: 在真实世界的家庭用电、共享出行和传感器数据交换数据集上进行了评估，可扩展到5600个代理和最多100万种解（暴力搜索）；典型运行情况下200个代理、100个决策属性的运行时间在30秒以内。

Conclusion: Argonaut是首个将系统构建、优化和分析统一在一个交互式循环中的工具，使分布式离散选择优化从一次性黑盒计算转变为人机交互过程。

Abstract: Distributed discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings is often hard to explore and navigate: disentangling what other agents choose, how their choices are interdependent, and how they collectively reach a global objective quickly becomes intractable as the system scales. The major limitation is observability of the search process. Existing methods are largely centralized and offer limited support, visualizing only the final solution or providing algorithm backends over a fixed dataset, so how a solution is reached stays a black box. We present Argonaut, a lightweight, containerized optimization dashboard that enables interactive, visual exploration of the entire search process for multi-agent discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings. Users upload datasets, construct agents and options, modify the decision space and its parameters on the fly, and run multiple algorithm backends to inspect how each configuration shapes local agent decisions and the resulting global objective. By uniting system construction, optimization, and analysis in one interactive loop, the first of its kind, Argonaut makes distributed discrete-choice optimization a human-in-the-loop process rather than a one-shot, black-box computation. We evaluate Argonaut on real-world household-electricity, shared-mobility, and sensor-data-exchange datasets scaling to 5600 agents and up to 1M solutions under brute force. Built on a Node.js interface with extensible Java and Python optimization backends, it maintains a typical runtime of 200 agents over 100 decision attributes in under 30 seconds.

</details>


### [411] [$Σ$-Mem: An Online Reliability Memory for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27958)
*Peilin Feng,Suorong Yang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Σ-Mem，一种在线可靠性记忆系统，用于多智能体LLM系统中记录个体智能体的历史能力证据和智能体间关系证据，并通过Weyl不等式保证谱变化有界，实现无需重训练的稳定在线适应。该记忆支持残差引导、无响应路由和可靠性加权投票三种通用接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统主要保存交互内容，而非建模哪些智能体可信及在何种条件下可信。在多智能体系统中，中心模型难以直接验证同伴的响应，这一局限性尤为突出。

Method: Σ-Mem将个体能力证据和同伴关系证据维护为实数对称状态，从决策后的正确性反馈中更新。利用Weyl不等式保证事件级更新的谱变化有界，实现无需重训练模型的稳定在线适应。提供统一的读写接口，支持残差引导、无响应同伴路由和可靠性加权投票。

Result: 在五个Qwen系列模型上，Σ-Mem能适应反事实可靠性变化，并泛化到未见过的智能体和任务域。直接记忆读取在完整OOD评估集上优于多数投票和最佳固定同伴。随着更多正确性反馈可用，性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 可靠性记忆可作为LLM多智能体系统中自适应协调的可复用基础，为长期智能体协作提供新的记忆范式。

Abstract: Memory is central to long-horizon LLM agents, yet existing memory systems primarily preserve interaction content rather than modeling which agents can be trusted and under what conditions. This limitation is particularly important in multi-agent systems, where a central model may be unable to directly verify plausible or correlated peer responses. We introduce $Σ$-Mem, an online reliability memory that records historical competence evidence for individual peers and peer relationship evidence across the peer set. Both forms of evidence are maintained as real symmetric states and updated from post-decision correctness feedback. By Weyl's inequality, the spectral change caused by each event-level update is bounded, enabling stable online adaptation without retraining the underlying models. $Σ$-Mem provides a general write-and-read interface: the same memory can be used for residual steering of a central model, response-free peer routing, or reliability-weighted voting. Across five Qwen-family models, $Σ$-Mem adapts to counterfactual reliability shifts and generalizes to unseen peers and task domains. Direct memory readouts also outperform majority voting and the best fixed peer over the full OOD evaluation set. Moreover, performance improves consistently as more correctness feedback becomes available, indicating that $Σ$-Mem progressively accumulates actionable reliability information. These results establish reliability memory as a reusable foundation for adaptive coordination in LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [412] [VISA: A Structured Description Protocol for Agent-Based Simulation Models Towards Machine Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28027)
*Zhou He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: VISA是一种结构化的、基于符号的智能体建模（ABM）描述协议，通过八张互联表格和十九条可执行一致性规则，使模型可被机器解析和验证，并利用三个LLM可执行技能（撰写、检查、代码生成）实现作者-检查-代码-复现全流程的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体模型（ABM）难以复现，因为其行为分散在散文叙述、平台特定代码和隐含假设中，导致不同读者从同一文档中重建出不同的模型。论文旨在解决这一可复现性危机。

Method: 提出VISA协议：用八个互联表格（代理层的Agent/Variable/Sensing/Internal Function，模型层的Associated Data/Input-Output/Schedule/Validation）描述模型；配套十九条可执行一致性规则将模型有效性转化为可检查属性；并设计三个LLM可执行的技能（撰写、检查、代码生成）来实现完整的作者-检查-代码-复现循环。

Result: 在三个外部独立撰写的ABM上验证：成功从VISA规格直接复现两个跨语言模型（NetLogo到Python）；第三个工业AnyLogic模型被完整捕获为八张表并通过十九条规则，同时诚实标注了因专有移动库和数据不可用导致的复现受阻。

Conclusion: VISA将复现障碍从不可见的模型中转移到已命名、可定位的依赖上，使其可操作化。这既是可复现性的提升，也是透明度方面的贡献。

Abstract: Agent-based models (ABMs) are difficult to reproduce: their behavior is spread across prose narratives, platform-specific code, and implicit assumptions, so that two readers routinely reconstruct different models from the same documentation. We present VISA, a structured, symbol-based description protocol that specifies a model in eight interconnected tables---four at the agent level (Agent, Variable, Sensing, Internal Function) and four at the model level (Associated Data, Input/Output, Schedule, Validation)---under the principle of minimality with completeness. VISA makes a model machine-parseable and unambiguous via two artifacts: nineteen executable consistency rules that turn model validity into a checkable property, and three reusable LLM-executable skills (authoring, checking, and code generation) that operationalize the full author--check--code--reproduce loop. We validate the protocol on three external, independently authored ABMs spanning three platforms: we reproduce two cross-language (NetLogo to Python) directly from their VISA specifications, and we capture a third, an industrial AnyLogic model, in eight tables (passing all nineteen rules) while honestly demarcating where reproduction is blocked by a proprietary movement library and unavailable data---itself a transparency contribution. VISA moves the reproduction barrier from the model, where it is invisible, to a named, localized dependency, where it is actionable.

</details>


### [413] [AgentRadio: Passive Awareness for Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28430)
*Xinxing Ren,Qianbo Zang,Ziyan Wang,Caelum Forder,Suman Deb,Peter Carroll,Zekun Guo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出AgentRadio异步消息传递层，使多编码智能体在任务执行过程中能实时共享发现，而无需等待阶段边界，在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上将任务解决率从单智能体的32.3%提升至62.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模代码库理解是长时程任务，单个LLM智能体难以独立完成（仅32.3%成功率）。现有的多智能体系统只在阶段边界进行通信，执行过程中的新发现无法及时共享，导致工作效率受限。

Method: 提出AgentRadio，一种异步消息传递层，为编码智能体工具提供三个原语：线程（threads）、消息（messages）和等待提及（waiting for mentions）。最后一种作为后台任务运行，在不中断前台工作的前提下暴露队友消息，使各智能体被动感知同伴发现并将其融入当前任务。在五阶段分工与协商协议下组织四个智能体协作。

Result: 在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上，四个AgentRadio组织的智能体解决了62.1%的任务，比单智能体高29.8个百分点，也超过使用更新版本Opus 4.8的Claude Code（57.2%）。规则级分析显示收益随任务难度增加而增长。

Conclusion: 异步、非阻塞的智能体间消息传递能在执行过程中实现有效的任务修正与协作，显著提升长时程代码理解任务的性能，且收益在困难任务上更为明显。

Abstract: Understanding large codebases is a long-horizon task for Large Language Model (LLM) agents: answering a single question can require building and running the software, tracing execution across files, and synthesizing evidence over tens of minutes. On SWE-Atlas QnA, a benchmark of long-horizon questions over production repositories, a single Claude Code agent (Opus 4.6) resolves only 32.3% of tasks. Dividing the work among agents with clean contexts mitigates this limitation. However, the subtasks of code comprehension are interdependent. One agent's findings can rewrite another's task, so agents must coordinate during execution, not only at phase boundaries. Existing multi-agent systems support such exchange only between phases, through staged handoffs or synchronized rounds. Communication and work remain mutually exclusive. A discovery made mid-execution cannot be shared until the next boundary. We present AgentRadio, an asynchronous message-passing layer that equips coding-agent harnesses with three primitives: threads, messages, and waiting for mentions. The last runs as a background task, surfacing teammates' messages without interrupting foreground work, so each agent remains passively aware of its peers and folds new findings into its ongoing task. Under a five-phase protocol of division of labor and negotiation, four agents organized by AgentRadio resolve 62.1% of tasks, 29.8 points above a single agent and above Claude Code with the newer Opus 4.8 (57.2%). Rubric-level analysis shows the gain growing with task difficulty, consistent with mid-course correction as the underlying mechanism. Our code is available at https://github.com/Coral-Protocol/AgentRadio.

</details>


### [414] [Using Theory of Mind to Arbitrate between Social and Non-social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28601)
*Lance Ying,Ryan Truong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出理性心智化模型，解释人类如何在社交学习与非社交学习之间做出权衡。该模型通过推理他人目标和行为的信息量来估算社交学习的效用，并在新游戏中定量捕捉了人类在这两种策略之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交学习是人类从他人那里获取知识的重要机制，但人们有时会选择直接经验而非社交学习。理解人们如何在社交与非社交学习之间进行权衡是一个关键问题，此前缺乏能够定量解释这种决策过程的计算模型。

Method: 提出Rational Mentalizing模型，该模型通过推理他人的目标及其未来行为的信息性来估算社交学习的效用，然后将社交学习效用与非社交学习效用进行比较。使用一个新颖的游戏实验，让玩家在观察其他代理与探索环境之间做出选择，以验证模型。

Result: Rational Mentalizing模型能够定量捕捉人类在社交与非社交学习策略之间的权衡行为。

Conclusion: 选择性社交学习由"心理理论"（Theory of Mind）驱动，服务于效用最大化的目标。

Abstract: Social learning is a powerful mechanism through which agents learn about the world from others. However, humans sometimes choose direct experience over social learning, which can carry time and cognitive resource costs. How do people balance social and non-social learning? We propose a Rational Mentalizing model of the decision to engage in social learning. This model estimates the utility of social learning by reasoning about another agent's goal and the informativeness of their future actions. It then weighs the utility of social learning against the utility of non-social learning. Using a novel game where players choose between observing other agents or exploring the environment, we show that the Rational Mentalizing model can quantitatively capture human trade-offs between these strategies. These findings suggest that selective social learning is guided by 'Theory of Mind' in the service of utility maximization.

</details>
